- **peekly-flow-reviewer**: Peekly 구현이 설계 문서의 확정 결정사항(5장)과 UX 계약을 실제로 지키는지, 그리고 자격증명 처리·프롬프트 인젝션 같은 보안 리스크가 없는지 검토한다. 여러 도구가 합쳐진 뒤 통합 검토가 필요하거나, 릴리즈 전 최종 점검을 할 때 사용. Proactively use after multiple Peekly components are implemented, before considering a feature/milestone done. Proactively use when implementing or modifying Peekly's MCP browser-automation, crawling, or cost-estimation tools. Proactively use when implementing or auditing macOS/Windows platform-specific code paths in Peekly. Proactively use when implementing or modifying Peekly's PPT report generation logic. - **pentest-business-logic**: Business-logic & workflow specialist in the A6 team. - **pentest-bypass-miner**: 威胁建模技能威胁收敛阶段·专项绕过专用子代理。对给定 project-id、目标漏洞类型与一批 url-id（≤5 个同类型 URL），针对各 URL 该类型 status=filtered（存在漏洞信号但防护绕不过）的条目穷尽多族绕过突破，某 URL 突破后在本批其他 URL 复用同法；突破则产出 VULN-VD 报告并更新矩阵。仅在 pentest-windftsy skill 威胁收敛阶段由主代理调度，输入 project-id + 漏洞类型 + 一批 url-id，其余信息子代理自取。 ([HeaSec/Pentest-WindFtsy](https://github.com/HeaSec/Pentest-WindFtsy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pentest-bypass-miner.md) · ★77 · 2 copies)
- **pentest-classifier**: Classification phase. Takes surface.json from pentest-recon and enriches each raw finding (open port + banner, tech fingerprint, nuclei match, interesting endpoint) with likely CVE IDs, CVSS v3.1 score, severity, attack category, false-positive probability, and chain candidates. Produces a classified_findings.json ranked by exploitability. ([subhoS/hunt](https://github.com/subhoS/hunt/blob/HEAD/skill/agents/pentest-classifier.md))
- **pentest-commander**: Pentest engagement lead. Owns research, planning, multi-domain grow-agent dispatch, cross-target synthesis, and the findings report. - **percpu-syscall-data**: Every CPU MUST have its own `SyscallCpuData` and `SyscallUserContext`. These are accessed via the `KERNEL_GS_BASE` MSR + `swapgs` at syscall entry. A single global for either data structure is a **fatal SMP bug**: two CPUs entering syscalls simultaneously clobber each other's saved registers. ([chris17453/oxide_os](https://github.com/chris17453/oxide_os/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/percpu-syscall-data.md) · ★3)
- **percy**: Senior performance engineer who reviews plans and diffs for performance and scalability — measurement-first, never speculation. Use when a plan or phase touches DB schema/query shapes, caching, pagination, hot-path endpoints, bundle-affecting frontend changes, or states a performance goal — and as the lead for performance/scaling audits. Findings must carry a measurement or a complexity argument tied to a named hot path; "this could be slow" is not a finding. Read-only on source; runs profilers, EXPLAIN, builds, and load probes to gather evidence. ([jstuart0/mozart-orchestration](https://github.com/jstuart0/mozart-orchestration/blob/HEAD/agents/percy.md) · ★1)

([tslateman/tempo](https://github.com/tslateman/tempo/blob/HEAD/agents/perf-analyzer.md))
- **perf-architect**: Use for throughput, latency, QoS, and scheduling exploration. Proposes and implements micro-architectural improvements: split outstanding read/write, concurrent_rw, WRR/DRR arbiters, posted writes, priority lanes. Always pairs a change with a perf-measurement delta AND the functional regression. Flags clearly when a change exits the no-ID single-outstanding model. ([BASHAR003/axi-crossbar-SoC-Bus-for-ANN-chip](https://github.com/BASHAR003/axi-crossbar-SoC-Bus-for-ANN-chip/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/perf-architect.md))
- **perf-architect**: Architecture engineer for performance and reliability audits. Finds the structural causes of perf problems — coupling that forces N+1, missing cache/pagination/boundary layers, unbounded growth designed into the system, sync-in-async, wrong data structures on hot paths, and chatty cross-boundary calls. Spawned by /perf-audit. Returns JSON findings against the panel contract. ([NarekManukyan/perf-audit](https://github.com/NarekManukyan/perf-audit/blob/HEAD/perf-audit/agents/perf-architect.md))
- **perf-architect**: Use when executing performance engineering tasks, building k6 scripts, running load tests, drafting telemetry reports, or preparing audit payloads for the bottleneck-auditor sub-agent. ([tiennt220805/performance-testing-skills](https://github.com/tiennt220805/performance-testing-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/perf-architect.md))
- **perf-attribution**: Performans atıf analisti — Multi-strategy hedge fund ilhamıyla. Engine bazlı P&L attribution, cannibalization analizi. "Kâr nereden geldi, zarar nereden?" sorusunu cevaplar. ([emirhan2823/myclaudeagents](https://github.com/emirhan2823/myclaudeagents/blob/HEAD/agents/perf-attribution.md))
- **perf-attribution-runner**: Runs Perfetto sweeps for /specs-lens-perf-optimize — captures baseline, targeted, or full-sweep traces against PerfAttributionHarness, screenshots harness markers, returns ms/frame + slice data. Read-only: does not edit project code or commit.
